MI0007

mobius_inversion_reconstructs_divisor_transform

Alpha v33 independently verified · alpha_closed; checked-use authorized; not Stable

The converse constructs actual unit tables and finite folds; associativity turns one times a Möbius convolution back into the original divisor transform.

Current library: Alpha v33, 4,092 checked-use theorems; Stable remains 432. Historical first admissions, original proof editions, and non-admitted aliases are preserved.
